913	    if (this.netPuppet) { this.netPuppetStep(); return; }
914	    if (--this.life <= 0) { this.dead = true; return; }

916	    this.handX = p.cx;
917	    this.handY = p.cy;
918	    // ChangeDir 段（:64925-64935,活跃/回收态公共段）:球相对玩家 X 定 direction,
919	    // ±2px 死区内保持原值——击退方向用 this.dir（非 sign(vx)）
920	    if (this.cx > p.cx + 2) this.dir = 1;
921	    else if (this.cx < p.cx - 2) this.dir = -1;
922	    this.hitCd.tick();

924	    const ms = p.attackSpeedMult || 1;

926	    let range = YOYO_RANGE[this.projId] ?? 200;
927	    if (p.equipStats.yoyoString) range = range * 1.25 + 30;  // 线圈 yoyoString(:64971)
928	    range /= msRange;
929	    let top = (YOYO_TOP[this.projId] ?? 10) / msRange;
930	    let inertia = Math.max(1, 14 - top / 2);   // num7(:64968-64971)
931	    const deadZone = 5 + top / 2;              // num9 光标死区半径(:64972)
932	    // 寿命（:64840-64854）：秒数 ÷(1+ms)/2,超 YoyosLifeTimeMultiplier → 回收态
933	    this.lifeT += 1 / 60 / ((1 + ms) / 2);
934	    let lifeMul = YOYO_LIFE[this.projId] ?? -1;
935	    if (p.equipStats.yoyoString && lifeMul > 0) lifeMul *= 1.5;
936	    const expired = lifeMul !== -1 && this.lifeT > lifeMul;
937	    if (this.recalled || !this.channel() || expired) {

956	    const cx = this.x + this.w / 2, cy = this.y + this.h / 2;
957	    const dPlayer = Math.hypot(p.cx - cx, p.cy - cy);

959	    if (dPlayer > range * 1.3) { this.recalled = true; return; } // flag4:1.3×超程强制回收(下帧走回收态)

971	      this.ai0 = tx; this.ai1 = ty;

1054	  img = new Image();
1055	  img.src = `sprites/vanilla/${name}.png`;
1056	  chainImgCache.set(name, img);
1057	  return img;
1058	}
1059	
1060	export class FlailProj extends Entity {
1061	  /** 原版碰撞盒按型号(Projectile.SetDefaults):247/757=34 / 947/948=20 /
1062	   *  1058=26 / 其余 22——写死 22 会让 5 个型号命中盒失真,2026-08-13 review */
1063	  w: number;
1064	  h: number;
1065	  damage: number;
1066	  knockback: number;
1067	  projId: number;
1068	  /** GetWeaponCrit 合并值（Game 侧传入；缺省 4 基础） */
1069	  critChance = 0.04;
1070	  /** ⑥ 护甲穿透（Game 注入，命中传 hurt 减防） */
1071	  armorPen = 0;
1072	  /** 近战系（吃武器浸剂） */
1073	  meleeProj = true;
1074	  /** channel 电平（mouseHeld && 手持原物品 && !uiBlocking）——Game 注入 */
1075	  private channel: () => boolean;
1076	  /** 鼠标世界坐标 getter（松手掷出方向） */
1077	  private target: () => { x: number; y: number };
1078	  private prm: FlailParams;
1079	  /** 子弹生成回调(Game 侧注入 entities.add——GameHooks 不暴露实体桶)。
1080	   *  AI_015 特例:247 Flairon 飞行中自机弹 248 / 757 Drippler 掷出回收气泡 928 /
1081	   *  1058 轨道刺 405 */
1082	  spawnProj: ((x: number, y: number, vx: number, vy: number, projId: number, dmg: number, kb: number) => void) | null = null;
1083	  /** 247/1058 周期计数(localAI[2]/localAI[0]) */
1084	  private subT = 0;
1085	  /** 757 过渡点缓存:[vx,vy] 待下帧掷气泡(掷出态 ×0.3 / 按住转垂地 ×0.2) */
1086	  private trans757: [number, number] | null = null;
1087	  /** ai[0] 状态：0 旋转 / 1 掷出 / 2 回拉 / 5 坠落 / 6 垂地悬荡 / 4 回收 */
1088	  private st = 0;
1089	  /** ai[1] 状态内计数 */
1090	  private stT = 0;
1091	  /** localAI[1] 旋转态累计 tick */
1092	  private spinT = 0;
1093	  /** localAI[0] 弹墙累计（AI_015_HandleMovementCollision:41006） */
1094	  private bounces = 0;
1095	  private rotation = 0;
1096	  private hitCd = new HitCooldown();
1097	  /** 手部坐标（链条起点，每 tick 刷新） */
1098	  handX = 0;
1099	  handY = 0;
1100	  life = 7200;
1101	  dead = false;
1102	
1103	  constructor(x: number, y: number, damage: number, knockback: number, projId: number,
1104	    channel: () => boolean, target: () => { x: number; y: number }) {
1105	    super();
1106	    this.x = x; this.y = y;
1107	    this.handX = x; this.handY = y;
1108	    this.damage = damage;
1109	    this.knockback = knockback;
1110	    this.projId = projId;
1111	    this.channel = channel;
1112	    this.target = target;
1113	    this.prm = { ...FLAIL_DEFAULT, ...FLAIL_PARAMS[projId] };
1114	    const pd = projectileData(projId);
1115	    this.w = pd?.width ?? 22;
1116	    this.h = pd?.height ?? 22;
1117	  }
1118	
1119	  draw(r: Renderer, cam: Camera): void {
1120	    const ctx = r.canvas.getContext('2d');
1121	    if (ctx) this.drawChain(ctx, r);
1122	    drawProj(r, cam, this, this.projId, this.rotation, '#E04040');
1123	  }
1124	
1125	  /** 链条（DrawProj_FlailChains Main.cs:34752-34842 1:1）：球心 → 手臂方向逐节
1126	   *  步长=链节高，每节按所在格光照着色；948 三段混排 / 757 六帧动画链 */
1127	  private drawChain(ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D, r: Renderer): void {
1128	    const img = chainImg(flailChainTex(this.projId));
1129	    if (!img || !img.complete || img.naturalWidth === 0) {
1130	      // 贴图未就绪兜底：细线（旧实现形态）
1131	      ctx.strokeStyle = '#605848';
1132	      ctx.lineWidth = 1.5;
1133	      ctx.beginPath();
1134	      ctx.moveTo(this.handX, this.handY);
1135	      ctx.lineTo(this.x + this.w / 2, this.y + this.h / 2);
1136	      ctx.stroke();
1137	      return;
1138	    }
1139	    // 光照采样（Lighting.GetColor 每节一次；拿不到则全亮。
1140	    // ★lightAt 返回 0..255(Renderer 同源 /255 消费)——不除即恒满亮的静默 bug,2026-08-13 review)
1141	    const lighting = (r as unknown as { lighting?: { lightAt: (tx: number, ty: number) => [number, number, number] } | null }).lighting;
1142	    const lightAt = (wx: number, wy: number): number => {
1143	      if (!lighting) return 1;
1144	      const [lr, lg, lb] = lighting.lightAt(Math.floor(wx / TILE), Math.floor(wy / TILE));
1145	      return Math.min(1, (lr * 0.3 + lg * 0.5 + lb * 0.2) / 255);
1146	    };
1147	    // 球心 → 臂方向（playerArmPosition.MoveTowards(center,4) 近似为手部）
1148	    const cx0 = this.x + this.w / 2, cy0 = this.y + this.h / 2;
1149	    let dx = this.handX - cx0, dy = this.handY - cy0;
1150	    const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
1151	    if (d < 1) return;
1152	    dx /= d; dy /= d;
1153	    const frames = this.projId === 757 ? 6 : 1;
1154	    const linkH = img.naturalHeight / frames + (this.projId === 757 ? -2 : 0);
1155	    const rotation = Math.atan2(dy, dx) + Math.PI / 2;
1156	    let px = cx0, py = cy0;
1157	    let i = 0;
1158	    for (let remain = d + linkH / 2; remain > 0; remain -= linkH, i++) {
1159	      let tex = img;
1160	      let sy = 0, sh = img.naturalHeight;
1161	      if (this.projId === 757) { sy = (i % 6) * (img.naturalHeight / 6); sh = img.naturalHeight / 6; }
1162	      else if (this.projId === 948) {
1163	        // 三段：前 4 节 Chain43（全亮）→ 2 节 Chain42（最低亮度 140）→ 其余 Chain41
1164	        if (i < 4) { tex = chainImg('Chain43') ?? img; }
1165	        else if (i < 6) { tex = chainImg('Chain42') ?? img; }
1166	      }
1167	      if (!tex.complete || tex.naturalWidth === 0) tex = img;
1168	      const alpha = this.projId === 948 && i < 4 ? 1
1169	        : this.projId === 948 && i < 6 ? Math.max(lightAt(px, py), 140 / 255)
1170	          : lightAt(px, py);
1171	      ctx.save();
1172	      ctx.translate(px, py);
1173	      ctx.rotate(rotation);
1174	      ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
1175	      ctx.globalAlpha = Math.max(0.15, alpha);
1176	      ctx.drawImage(tex, 0, sy, tex.naturalWidth, sh, -tex.naturalWidth / 2, -sh / 2, tex.naturalWidth, sh);
1177	      ctx.restore();
1178	      px += dx * linkH;
1179	      py += dy * linkH;
1180	    }
1181	  }
1182	
1183	  fixedUpdate(_dt: number, game: GameHooks): void {
1184	    if (--this.life <= 0) { this.dead = true; return; }
1185	    // 火焰重锤 948 入水(非岩浆)→ 变型 948→947(:41069-41072 type==948 && wet && !lavaWet)
1186	    if (this.projId === 948) {
1187	      const st948 = game.world.store;
1188	      const li = st948.liquid[st948.idx(Math.floor((this.x + this.w / 2) / TILE), Math.floor((this.y + this.h / 2) / TILE))];
1189	      if (li > 64 && st948.liquidType[st948.idx(Math.floor((this.x + this.w / 2) / TILE), Math.floor((this.y + this.h / 2) / TILE))] !== 2) {
1190	        this.projId = 947;
1191	      }
1192	    }
1193	    const p = game.player;
1194	    this.handX = p.cx;
1195	    this.handY = p.cy;
1196	    this.hitCd.tick();
1197	    // meleeSpeed 缩放链（:41116-41124,1/meleeSpeed）——repo attackSpeedMult 语义同向(>1 更快)
1198	    const inv = 1 / (p.attackSpeedMult || 1);
1199	    const throwSpd = this.prm.throwSpeed * inv;
1200	    const pullAccel = this.prm.pullAccel * inv;
1201	    const retractKill = this.prm.retractKill * inv;
1202	    const returnAccel = this.prm.returnAccel * inv;
1203	    const returnKill = this.prm.returnKill * inv;
1204	    const farPull = this.prm.farPullSpeed * inv;
1205	    const maxRange = 800;
1206	    // 击杀门（:41053-41060）：主角死亡/离手 900×(1/meleeSpeed)
1207	    const distHand = () => Math.hypot(this.x + this.w / 2 - p.cx, this.y + this.h / 2 - p.cy);
1208	    if (p.dead || distHand() > 900 * inv) { this.dead = true; return; }

1210	    // num17/num18(:41129-41130):链长上限=掷速×飞行tick;垂地悬荡放行 +160

1216	        if (!held) {

1218	          const t = this.target();
1219	          const dx = t.x - p.cx, dy = t.y - p.cy;
1220	          const dd = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
1221	          this.vx = (dx / dd) * throwSpd + p.vx;
1222	          this.vy = (dy / dd) * throwSpd + p.vy;

1224	          this.y = p.cy - this.h / 2;

1228	        }

1231	        // Y×0.8;向下(gravDir>0)再减半——扁椭圆轨迹,上快下慢。
1232	        // direction 取朝鼠标侧（原版 ChangeDir(vector5.X>0?1:-1) :41203;不直接改
1233	        // player.facing,登记 GAP）
1234	        const t0 = this.target();
1235	        const dir = (t0.x >= p.cx ? 1 : -1);

1237	        let ox = Math.cos(a) * dir, oy = Math.sin(a) * dir;

1239	        if (oy > 0) oy *= 0.5;

1260	      }

1289	      }
1290	      case 5: { // 坠落态（:41346-41362,撞墙入口见下方 tile 碰撞）：重力坠地
1291	        this.stT++;

1295	        this.vx *= 0.95;

1297	      }

1300	        this.vy += 0.8;
1301	        this.vx *= 0.95;
1302	        break;
1303	      }
1304	      case 4: { // 回收态（:41292-41234 区段 case 4:41321-41344）：无视碰撞回手

1310	        const hx = p.cx - tx2, hy = p.cy - ty2;

1708	  vx: number;
1709	  vy: number;

1713	  timeLeft: number;
1714	  private ai0 = 0;
1715	  private rot = 0;

1718	  private fairyT = 0;
1719	
1720	  constructor(x: number, y: number, vx: number, vy: number, projId: number) {
1721	    super();
1722	    this.x = x; this.y = y;
1723	    this.vx = vx; this.vy = vy;
1724	    this.projId = projId;
1725	    this.w = projId === 473 ? 8 : 6;
1726	    this.h = this.w;
1727	    this.timeLeft = projId === 473 ? 3600 : 9000;
1728	  }
1729	
1730	  draw(r: Renderer, _cam: Camera): void {
1731	    // 原版投射物绘制尺寸 = 贴图原生尺寸 × scale（碰撞盒 6×6 与绘制解耦）——
1732	    // 50/53/515/1089 贴图是 22×6 横条（此前按碰撞盒 6px 画成 1.6px 细丝）；
1733	    // 870 妖精 = 18×96 纵条 6 帧（帧进 5t）；473 = 18×18 单帧
1734	    const ctx = r.canvas.getContext('2d');

1736	    const img = projSprite(this.projId);

1739	    ctx.rotate(this.rot);

1745	      } else {
1746	        ctx.drawImage(img, -img.naturalWidth / 2, -img.naturalHeight / 2);
1747	      }
1748	    } else {
1749	      ctx.fillStyle = '#7FE8A8';
1750	      ctx.fillRect(-3, -3, 6, 6);
1751	    }
1752	    ctx.restore();
1753	  }

1755	  fixedUpdate(_dt: number, game: GameHooks): void {
1756	    if (--this.timeLeft <= 0) { this.dead = true; return; }
1757	    const st = game.world.store;
1758	    const solidAt = (px: number, py: number) => {
1759	      const tx = Math.floor(px / TILE), ty = Math.floor(py / TILE);
1760	      return st.inBounds(tx, ty) && st.isSolid(tx, ty);
1761	    };
1762	    if (this.fairy) {
1763	      // AI_170_FairyGlowstick（cs:42997-43053）：排斥场 + 衰减
1764	      this.fairyT++;
1765	      if (this.fairyT % 10 === 0 && Math.hypot(this.vx, this.vy) > 1) {
1766	        const ptx = Math.floor((this.x + this.w / 2) / TILE), pty = Math.floor((this.y + this.h / 2) / TILE);
1767	        let zx = 0, zy = 0, n = 0;
1768	        for (let i = ptx - 10; i <= ptx + 10; i++) {
1769	          for (let j = pty - 10; j <= pty + 10; j++) {
1770	            if (!st.inBounds(i, j) || !st.isSolid(i, j)) continue;
1771	            const dx = ptx - i, dy = pty - j;
1772	            const len = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
1773	            const f = Math.max(10 - len, 0) / len * 3;
1774	            zx += dx * f; zy += dy * f; n++;
1775	          }
1776	        }
1777	        if (n > 0) { zx /= n; zy /= n; }
1778	        if (Math.hypot(zx, zy) > 1) { this.vx = zx; this.vy = zy; }
1779	      }
1780	      this.vx *= 0.95; this.vy *= 0.95;
1781	      if (Math.hypot(this.vx, this.vy) < 1) { this.vx = 0; this.vy = 0; }
1782	      this.x += this.vx; this.y += this.vy;
1783	      return;
1784	    }
1785	    // AI_014 主体
1786	    this.ai0++;
1787	    if (this.ai0 > 5) {
1788	      this.ai0 = 5;
1789	      // 着地判定用上一帧 vy 是否未变（原版 velocity.Y == 0 即已落地）——落地格 vy 被置 0
1790	      if (this.vy === 0 && this.vx !== 0) {
1791	        this.vx *= 0.97;
1792	        if (Math.abs(this.vx) < 0.01) this.vx = 0;
1793	      }
1794	      this.vy += 0.2;
1795	    }
1796	    this.rot += this.vx * 0.1;
1797	    // 粘性（53）：tileCollide=false，与实心重叠 → 粘住
1798	    if (this.projId === 53) {
1799	      const overlap = solidAt(this.x + this.w / 2, this.y + this.h / 2)
1800	        || solidAt(this.x + 1, this.y + 1) || solidAt(this.x + this.w - 2, this.y + this.h - 2);
1801	      if (overlap) { this.vx = 0; this.vy = -0.2; return; }
1802	      this.x += this.vx; this.y += this.vy;
1803	      return;
1804	    }
1805	    // 移动 + 碰撞反弹（反弹表 cs:18261-18333）
1806	    const lastVx = this.vx, lastVy = this.vy;
1807	    let nx = this.x + this.vx, ny = this.y + this.vy;
1808	    let hitX = false, hitY = false;
1809	    if (solidAt(nx + this.w / 2, this.y + this.h / 2) || solidAt(nx + this.w / 2, this.y + 1)) { hitX = true; nx = this.x; }
1810	    if (solidAt(nx + this.w / 2, ny + this.h) || solidAt(nx + 1, ny + this.h) || solidAt(nx + this.w - 1, ny + this.h)) { hitY = true; ny = this.y; }
1811	    if (hitX || hitY) {

1814	        this.vx = -lastVx; this.vy = -lastVy;
1815	        this.fairy = true;
1816	        this.fairyT = 0;
1817	        this.rot = 0;
1818	        return;
1819	      }
1820	      if (this.projId === 50) {
1821	        if (hitX) this.vx = lastVx * -0.2;
1822	        if (hitY) this.vy = lastVy > 1.5 ? lastVy * -0.2 : 0;
1823	      } else if (this.projId >= 1089) {

1825	        if (hitX) this.vx = Math.abs(this.vx - lastVx) < 1 ? (lastVx < 0 ? -1 : 1) : -lastVx * 0.99;
1826	        if (hitY) this.vy = -lastVy * 0.99;
1827	      } else {

1831	      }
1832	    }
1833	    this.x = nx; this.y = ny;
1834	  }
1835	}

	private void CreateImpactExplosion(int dustAmountMultiplier, Vector2 explosionOrigin, ref Point scanAreaStart, ref Point scanAreaEnd, int explosionRange, out bool causedShockwaves)
	{
		causedShockwaves = false;
		int num = 4;
		for (int i = scanAreaStart.X; i <= scanAreaEnd.X; i++)
		{
			for (int j = scanAreaStart.Y; j <= scanAreaEnd.Y; j++)
			{
				if (Vector2.Distance(explosionOrigin, new Vector2(i * 16, j * 16)) > (float)explosionRange)
				{
					continue;
				}
				Tile tileSafely = Framing.GetTileSafely(i, j);
				if (!tileSafely.active() || !Main.tileSolid[tileSafely.type] || Main.tileSolidTop[tileSafely.type] || Main.tileFrameImportant[tileSafely.type])
				{
					continue;
				}
				Tile tileSafely2 = Framing.GetTileSafely(i, j - 1);
				if (tileSafely2.active() && Main.tileSolid[tileSafely2.type] && !Main.tileSolidTop[tileSafely2.type])
				{
					continue;
				}
				int num2 = WorldGen.KillTile_GetTileDustAmount(fail: true, tileSafely) * dustAmountMultiplier;
				for (int k = 0; k < num2; k++)
				{
					Dust obj = Main.dust[WorldGen.KillTile_MakeTileDust(i, j, tileSafely)];
					obj.velocity.Y -= 3f + (float)num * 1.5f;
					obj.velocity.Y *= Main.rand.NextFloat();
					obj.scale += (float)num * 0.03f;
				}
				if (num >= 2)
				{
					for (int l = 0; l < num2 - 1; l++)
					{
						Dust obj2 = Main.dust[WorldGen.KillTile_MakeTileDust(i, j, tileSafely)];
						obj2.velocity.Y -= 1f + (float)num;
						obj2.velocity.Y *= Main.rand.NextFloat();
					}
				}
				if (num2 > 0)
				{
					causedShockwaves = true;
				}
			}
		}
	}

`DirectionFrom(target)` = "the direction from target to this"? In XNA Terraria, `Entity.DirectionFrom(Vector2 Source)` returns `((this.position + size/2) - Source).SafeNormalize()`. Let me verify. Actually in Terraria's Utils/Entity: `public Vector2 DirectionTo(Vector2 Destination) => (Destination - Center).SafeNormalize(default); public Vector2 DirectionFrom(Vector2 Source) => (Center - Source).SafeNormalize(default);` So DirectionFrom(mountedCenter) = (ball.Center - player.MountedCenter) normalized — pointing from player to ball. So itemRotation = angle from player to ball; and if ball is left of player (Center.X < mountedCenter.X) → itemRotation += π (wrap). This makes the held item point at the ball with correct flip.

And `player.heldProj = whoAmI` — marks "I'm holding this projectile" which affects the held item drawing: in vanilla, when heldProj != -1, the player draws the item using itemRotation (item is held pointing along itemRotation), instead of swinging. This is the "手臂指向" (arm pointing).

			channel = false;
		}

24	  // mirror=旋转坐标系内水平镜像（XNA dir=FlipHorizontally 等价，绕 origin 翻转）
25	  const img0 = projSprite(projId);
26	  const natural = drawSize < 0 && img0 && img0.complete && img0.naturalWidth > 0;
27	  const dw = natural ? img0!.naturalWidth : drawSize > 0 ? drawSize : e.w;
28	  const ctx = r.canvas.getContext('2d');
29	  if (!ctx) return;
30	  const img = img0;
31	  ctx.save();
32	  ctx.globalAlpha = alpha; // AI_161 细剑淡入淡出(:44993-44994)
33	  // y 中心用 h（342 冰霜刃 22×2 薄刃命中盒等非方形型号,勿用 w 兜）
34	  ctx.translate(e.x + e.w / 2, e.y + e.h / 2);
35	  ctx.rotate(ang);
36	  if (mirror) ctx.scale(-1, 1);
37	  ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
38	  if (img && img.complete && img.naturalWidth > 0) {
39	    ctx.drawImage(img, -dw / 2, -dw / 2, dw, dw * (img.naturalHeight / img.naturalWidth));
40	  } else {
41	    ctx.fillStyle = fallbackColor;
42	    ctx.fillRect(-dw / 2, -dw / 2, dw, dw);
43	  }
44	  ctx.restore();
45	}
46	
47	/** 同敌命中去重 + 冷却的通用伤害结算。
48	 *  hitCd 记录每敌剩余冷却 tick，每 fixedUpdate 调用方先 tickCd() 递减；
49	 *  cooldown >= 9999 视作单次结算（突刺类：一击每敌只中一次） */
50	class HitCooldown {
51	  private map = new Map<number, number>();
52	  tick(): void {
53	    for (const [k, v] of this.map) {
54	      if (v <= 1) this.map.delete(k);
55	      else this.map.set(k, v - 1);
56	    }
57	  }
58	  ready(id: number, cooldown: number): boolean {
59	    if (this.map.has(id)) return false;
60	    if (cooldown < 9999) this.map.set(id, cooldown);
61	    else this.map.set(id, 2147483647); // 永不再中
62	    return true;
63	  }
64	  clear(): void { this.map.clear(); }
65	}
66	
67	type ReflectableWeaponProj = {
68	  x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number;
69	  vx: number; vy: number;
70	  damage: number; knockback: number;
71	  projId?: number;
72	  hostile?: boolean;
73	  reflected?: boolean;

77	  meleeProj?: boolean;
78	};
79	
80	function hitEnemies(
81	  self: { x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number; vx: number; damage: number; knockback: number },
82	  game: GameHooks,
83	  hitCd: HitCooldown,

85	  dirX: number,
86	  pen = 0,
87	  /** 每次命中敌结算后的回调（Boomerang 的 Projectile.Damage 回收门 :12278-12291 用） */
88	  onHit?: () => void,
89	): void {
90	  // 小动物:一击致死(原版小动物是 NPC;近战挥砍同语义)。回旋镖/悠悠球等穿透型
91	  // 命中小动物不消耗穿透语义——hurt 自带 iframes 防同帧连击
92	  hitCritters(self, game);
93	  // 城镇 NPC 受击(原版 Projectile.Damage 对 friendly NPC 同样生效,杀裁缝师即此链);
94	  // 穿透型不消耗穿透语义(NPC.hurt 8t iframes 自去重)
95	  hitTownNpcs(self, game, self.damage, Math.sign(self.vx) * 2);
96	  const me = self as ReflectableWeaponProj;
97	  // 反射后的弹体 friendly 已翻——不再入敌怪判定（原版 Damage NPC 需 friendly 门禁）
98	  if (me.reflected) return;
99	  for (const ent of game.enemies()) {

101	    if (e.dead) continue;

119	    if (me.meleeProj) {

So for direction=1: angle goes 0 → π*10 over 60 ticks (5 full turns per second), rotating CCW... in screen coords with y down, positive angle rotation appears clockwise? XNA RotatedBy uses standard rotation matrix: (x cosθ - y sinθ, x sinθ + y cosθ). With y down, positive θ rotates from +X toward +Y (downward) — visually clockwise. ```ts
/** 掷出态撞墙冲击波(:41031-41034 → CreateImpactExplosion:66367-66412 +
 *  CreateImpactExplosion2_FlailTileCollision:66452-66500)。
 *  原版 = 命中盒内逐格(KillTile_GetTileDustAmount×2 带块色上抛尘,块上方需空气)
 *  + 8 颗椭圆(7×0.7)白尘环(速度 += 撞前速度方向×2,无块尘时再 8 颗 α100)。
 *  近似:粒子系统无逐格块色尘 → 上抛尘团 + 椭圆环两段(spawnBurst yMul 0.7) */
private impactExplosion(game: GameHooks, lvx: number, lvy: number): void {
  const cx0 = this.x + this.w / 2, cy0 = this.y + this.h / 2;
  // 块面尘(CreateImpactExplosion,乘数 2):命中盒所在格有实心面 → 上抛尘团
  game.spawnParticles(cx0, cy0 + this.h / 2, '#A09080', 6, 2.2, { life: 22, grav: 0.12, size: 2 });
  // 冲击波环(8 颗椭圆尘,速度沿撞前方向 ×2)
  const d = Math.hypot(lvx, lvy) || 1;
  game.spawnBurst?.(cx0, cy0, '#E8E4DC', 8, 2, Math.atan2(lvy, lvx), Math.PI, { life: 24, grav: -0.02, yMul: 0.7 });
}
```

Hmm, spawnBurst's `dir`/`spread` semantics: "dir 为中心角、spread 为半散布" — so spread=π gives full circle. Vanilla scatters too (base dust velocity is random from NewDustDirect) but with a +2 bias along the impact direction. So: spawnBurst(x, y, 8, speed≈1.5-2, dir=impactAngle, spread=π*0.9, yMul 0.7) is a decent approximation. Hmm, but wait: for a wall hit, the impact direction is INTO the wall — vanilla dust still moves along +velocityBefore (into the wall) which looks odd but that's what it does (dust clips into the wall). So a tiny amount (liquid=1) at the tile bottom would count if the probe box reaches it. `> 64` requires ≥ 1/4 tile. It's a coarse approximation — for a flail entering water, liquid at the surface tile is usually full (255) so >64 triggers immediately at the surface... Actually at the surface tile, the liquid might be partial. E.g., a half-full surface tile has liquid=128 > 64 ✓. A tile with liquid=50 (20% full) — vanilla would count as wet when the ball dips into the liquid portion; repo wouldn't. Minor. I'll keep 64 but document with the exact vanilla anchor (WetCollision :1575-1651, liquid>0 + 表面偏移 (256-liquid)/32×2).

Hmm, should I improve it to `li > 0`? The surface-offset means the liquid only occupies the bottom `(liquid/256)*16` px of the tile... wait: `num5 = (256 - liquid)/32; vector2.Y += num5*2` → the liquid top is at `j*16 + (256-liquid)/16` px, i.e., liquid fills from `j*16 + (256-liquid)/16` down to `j*16+16`. For liquid=64: top at j*16+12 — 4px of liquid at the tile bottom. So vanilla's wet for a ball center in that tile = only if the probe box overlaps the bottom 4px. The repo samples the ball CENTER tile and requires li > 64 — for a 22px ball, the center enters the liquid when the ball is ≥ (16-4)/16... rough. Keep 64, document. Not worth more precision given the particle-grade approximation elsewhere.

inLiquid helper:
```ts
  /** 中心格液体探测:类型省略 = 任一液体;type 见 LIQUID_TYPE(1水/2岩浆/3蜂蜜/4微光)。
   *  近似 Collision.WetCollision/LavaCollision(:1575-1651)的中心探盒 */
  private inLiquid(game: GameHooks, type?: number): boolean {
    const st = game.world.store;
    const i = st.idx(Math.floor((this.x + this.w / 2) / TILE), Math.floor((this.y + this.h / 2) / TILE));
    return st.liquid[i] > 64 && (type === undefined || st.liquidType[i] === type);
  }
```

        const x1 = Math.floor((x + this.w - 1) / TILE), y1 = Math.floor((y + this.h - 1) / TILE);
        for (let ty = y0; ty <= y1; ty++) for (let tx = x0; tx <= x1; tx++) {
          if (world.store.inBounds(tx, ty) && world.store.isSolid(tx, ty)) return true;
        }
        return false;
      };
      const lvx = this.vx, lvy = this.vy;
      const nx = this.x + this.vx;
      if (solid(nx, this.y)) {
        this.vx = -lvx * rest;
        this.bounces++; // localAI[0]:每次接触都计(:41001/:41010)——只计 >4 会让缓磨墙永不触发逃生
        if (Math.abs(lvx) > 4) this.hitTileFx(game, nx, this.y); // num2>0 门=音尘(:40999)
      } else this.x = nx;
      const ny = this.y + this.vy;
      if (solid(this.x, ny)) {
        this.vy = -lvy * rest;
        this.bounces++;
        if (Math.abs(lvy) > 4) this.hitTileFx(game, this.x, ny);
      } else this.y = ny;
      // 掷出态撞墙 → 坠落态（:41027-41037 + CreateImpactExplosion 音尘近似）
      if (this.st === 1 && (this.vx !== lvx || this.vy !== lvy)) {
        this.st = 5; this.stT = 0;
      }
      // 卡死逃生（:41040-41045）：弹墙≥10 次且非 3/0/5/6 态 → 强制回收
      if (this.st !== 3 && this.st !== 0 && this.st !== 5 && this.st !== 6 && this.bounces >= 10) {
        this.st = 4; this.stT = 0;
      }
    }
```
new:
```
    // 位移 + tile 碰撞（AI_015_HandleMovementCollision :40982-41049）：
    // 旋转态位置由 AI 直接钉死不积分;回收态 tileCollide=false
    if (this.st !== 0 && this.st !== 4) {
      const rest = this.st === 1 || this.st === 5 ? 0.4 : this.st === 6 ? 0 : 0.2;
      const world = game.world;
      const solid = (x: number, y: number): boolean => {
        const x0 = Math.floor(x / TILE), y0 = Math.floor(y / TILE);
        const x1 = Math.floor((x + this.w - 1) / TILE), y1 = Math.floor((y + this.h - 1) / TILE);
        for (let ty = y0; ty <= y1; ty++) for (let tx = x0; tx <= x1; tx++) {
          if (world.store.inBounds(tx, ty) && world.store.isSolid(tx, ty)) return true;
        }
        return false;
      };
      const lvx = this.vx, lvy = this.vy;
      // num2(:40998-41010):0=无音尘 / 1=|lastV|>4 按轴置位 / 掷出态强制 2(:41030)。
      // 音尘按 num2 次 HitTiles + 一次 SoundID 0(:41040-41045)——尘份量随档,音只一响
      let dustN = 0;
      const nx = this.x + this.vx;
      if (solid(nx, this.y)) {
        this.vx = -lvx * rest;
        this.bounces++; // localAI[0]:每次接触都计(:41001/:41010)——只计 >4 会让缓磨墙永不触发逃生
        if (Math.abs(lvx) > 4) dustN = 1; // num2>0 门(:40999)
      } else this.x = nx;
      const ny = this.y + this.vy;
      if (solid(this.x, ny)) {
        this.vy = -lvy * rest;
        this.bounces++;
        if (Math.abs(lvy) > 4) dustN = 1;
      } else this.y = ny;
      // 掷出态撞墙 → 坠落态（:41027-41037）:位移回卷 + num2 强制 2 + 冲击波(GAP#8 ④)
      if (this.st === 1 && (this.vx !== lvx || this.vy !== lvy)) {
        this.st = 5; this.stT = 0;
        this.x -= lvx; this.y -= lvy; // position -= vector(:41035)——不陷墙
        dustN = 2;
        this.impactExplosion(game, lvx, lvy);
      }
      if (dustN > 0) this.hitTileFx(game, this.x, this.y, dustN);
      // 卡死逃生（:41040-41045）：弹墙≥10 次且非 3/0/5/6 态 → 强制回收
      if (this.st !== 3 && this.st !== 0 && this.st !== 5 && this.st !== 6 && this.bounces >= 10) {
        this.st = 4; this.stT = 0;
      }
    }

new:
```
    // 旋转（:41476-41484 flag 通用段）：速度>1 对齐速度向,否则按 vx 缓转。
    // 247/1058 的 type 段内置 flag=false(:41437/:41388)——通用段被跳过,走各自专属式
    if (this.projId === 247) {
      // :41438-41442:rotation ±=(|vx|+|vy|)×0.01(按 vx 符号);旋转态再 +2π/15×direction
      const n22 = (Math.abs(this.vx) + Math.abs(this.vy)) * 0.01;
      this.rotation += this.vx > 0 ? n22 : -n22;
      if (this.st === 0) this.rotation += (Math.PI * 2 / 15) * p.facing;
    } else if (this.projId === 1058) {
      // :41389-41397:rotation 恒指玩家(-π/2 补偿);direction=(player.Center-ball.Center).X<0?1:-1
      this.rotation = Math.atan2(p.cy - this.cy, p.cx - this.cx) - Math.PI / 2;
    } else {
      const spd = Math.hypot(this.vx, this.vy);
      if (spd > 1) this.rotation = Math.atan2(this.vy, this.vx) + this.vx * 0.1;
      else this.rotation += this.vx * 0.1;
    }
    // 1058 出场淡入(:41385-41389,SetDefaults alpha=255 :9719)
    if (this.alpha > 0) this.alpha = Math.max(0, this.alpha - 50);

old:
```
  /** 撞墙反馈（Collision.HitTiles 尘 + SoundID 0 挖掘音——:41045 num2>0 门） */
  private hitTileFx(game: GameHooks, hx: number, hy: number): void {
    game.spawnParticles(hx + this.w / 2, hy + this.h / 2, '#A09080', 3, 1.5, { life: 14, size: 2 });

```
new:
```
  /** Player.ChangeDir（Player.cs:41440-41460）:同向早退只写 direction;手臂/道具镜像
   *  (compositeBackArm/itemRotation/itemLocation)本仓无对应系统,仅落 facing */
  private changeDir(p: { facing: number }, dir: number): void {
    if (p.facing !== dir) p.facing = dir;
  }

  /** 中心格液体探测:type 省略=任一液体;1水/2岩浆/3蜂蜜/4微光(LIQUID_TYPE)。
   *  近似 Collision.WetCollision(:1575-1651)中心探盒:阈值 64(≈1/4 格)近似原版
   *  表面偏移 (256-liquid)/16 的交叠判定(:1613-1616) */
  private inLiquid(game: GameHooks, type?: number): boolean {
    const st = game.world.store;
    const i = st.idx(Math.floor((this.x + this.w / 2) / TILE), Math.floor((this.y + this.h / 2) / TILE));
    return st.liquid[i] > 64 && (type === undefined || st.liquidType[i] === type);
  }

  /** 撞墙反馈(num2 次 Collision.HitTiles 尘 + 一次 SoundID 0 挖掘音,:41040-41045) */
  private hitTileFx(game: GameHooks, hx: number, hy: number, n = 1): void {
    game.spawnParticles(hx + this.w / 2, hy + this.h / 2, '#A09080', 3 * n, 1.5, { life: 14, size: 2 });
    game.playSfx('dig', 1, hx + this.w / 2, hy + this.h / 2);
  }

  /** 掷出态撞墙冲击波(GAP#8 ④)。原版两连(:41031-41034):
   *  CreateImpactExplosion(:66367-66412)——命中盒内逐格,实心且上方空气的块按
   *  KillTile_GetTileDustAmount×2 出【块色】上抛尘(速度 Y-=(3+num×1.5)×rand,
   *  scale+num×0.03;num 恒 4),出过尘 → causedShockwaves;
   *  CreateImpactExplosion2_FlailTileCollision(:66452-66500)——8 颗椭圆(7×0.7)白尘
   *  环(α=causedShockwaves?0:50,速度 Y-0.8/X×0.8 后 += 撞前速度向×2,scale 0.4),
   *  无块尘时再补 8 颗(α100,速度向×1.5)。
   *  近似:粒子系统无逐格块色/定点椭圆环 → 上抛尘团 + spawnBurst yMul 0.7 定向环 */
  private impactExplosion(game: GameHooks, lvx: number, lvy: number): void {
    const cx0 = this.x + this.w / 2, cy0 = this.y + this.h / 2;
    game.spawnParticles(cx0, cy0, '#A09080', 8, 2.5, { life: 22, grav: 0.1, size: 2 });
    const ang = Math.atan2(lvy, lvx);
    game.spawnBurst?.(cx0, cy0, '#E8E4DC', 8, 2, ang, Math.PI, { life: 24, grav: -0.02, yMul: 0.7 });
  }
```

Hmm — the spawnBurst dir: vanilla dust velocity += normalize(velocityBefore)×2 — i.e., along the impact direction (into the wall). Using `ang` = impact direction with spread π gives an omni ring biased... actually spawnBurst with spread=π is fully omni (dir ± π) — no bias at all. To bias along the impact direction I'd want spread smaller, e.g., spread = π/2 around ang. Hmm, but then it's a directional cone into the wall. Vanilla: random dust velocity + (impact dir × 2) — net bias INTO the wall by 2px/t. A cone along `ang` with spread π/2, speed ~2 is closer to the bias. But dust flying into the wall looks wrong visually (they'd clip into solid tiles — in vanilla they do too, briefly). Hmm. In vanilla the dust positions are ON the ellipse around the ball center which is at the wall surface... 

Let me simplify: half of the ring (the reflected side) reads better AND is arguably closer to what's visible (dust that flies into the wall is hidden). I'll use the reflected direction: ang + π (back toward the thrower) with spread π/2, yMul 0.7. And document that the into-wall half is dropped (hidden in vanilla too). Hmm, but "1:1" discipline... The task says "补音效/尘形状" — improve the dust shape. I'll do the ring with the reflected bias and note the deviation honestly in the comment: 原版速度偏置沿撞前方向(入墙半环被墙遮住,此处取反射半环近似).

Actually, you know what — let me keep it simple and defensible: spawn 8 dusts omni (spread π) at speed 2 with yMul 0.7 — matching the 8-count ellipse ring, and add the upward drift via grav negative... The yMul 0.7 in spawnBurst compresses the Y velocity component, which matches the ellipse's Y compression (0.7). I'll go with the reflected cone to make it visible. Final: `game.spawnBurst?.(cx0, cy0, '#E8E4DC', 8, 2, Math.atan2(-lvy, -lvx), Math.PI / 2, { life: 24, grav: -0.02, yMul: 0.7 })`.

Hmm, hold on: yMul applies to vy = sin(a)*sp*0.7 — that compresses vertical velocity. Vanilla ellipse: positions (7cos, 7×0.7 sin). Whatever — approximation, documented.

Now, also: should impactExplosion fire the dig sound? No — that's hitTileFx (num2 gate) which fires separately with dustN=2. ✓ vanilla: sound once via the num2>0 block ✓.

  target = { x: player.cx - 300, y: player.cy };
  run(f, hooks, 5);
  expect(player.facing).toBe(-1);  // 鼠标在左 → 朝左
  // 掷出态:球在右侧 → 面朝球(朝右)

});

}

    this.wire[i] = b;
    if (this.netReporter && !this.netSuppress) this.netReporter({ a: TileOpAction.SetActuated, x, y, v: v ? 1 : 0, fx: 0, fy: 0 });

  setLiquid(x: number, y: number, amount: number, type: number) {
    if (!this.inBounds(x, y)) return;
    const i = this.idx(x, y);
    const a = Math.max(0, Math.min(255, amount | 0));
    if (this.liquid[i] === a && this.liquidType[i] === type) return;
    this.liquid[i] = a;
    this.liquidType[i] = type;
    this.liquidListeners.forEach((fn) => fn(x, y));
  }
}

```

   *  0.12 已经 vanilla-accfx.json 走 equipStats.meleeSpeed，不应再翻倍。 */
  get attackSpeedMult(): number {
    const raw = (1 + this.equipStats.meleeSpeed)
      * (this.buffs.has(BuffType.Tipsy) ? 1.1 : 1)
      * (this.buffs.has(BuffType.Werewolf) ? 1.051 : 1)      // 狼人 meleeSpeed += 5.1%（:11489）
      * (this.buffs.has(BuffType.Weak) ? 0.949 : 1)          // 虚弱 meleeSpeed -= 5.1%（:11507）
      * (1 + 0.1 * this.beetleOrbs)                          // 甲虫攻击球攻速（:10071+）
      * (1 + this.buffs.wellFedMeleeSpeed);                  // 食物近战速 +5/7.5/10%（:11528+）
    return raw > 3 ? 1 / 3 : 1 / raw;                        // :28566-28573
  }

```

35	  const sunfuryId = window.__swItems['vi_220_sunfury'];
36	  g.player.inv.add(sunfuryId, 1);
37	  const slots = g.player.inv.slots;
38	  const bi = slots.findIndex((it) => it && it.id === sunfuryId);
39	  if (bi > 0) { const t = slots[0]; slots[0] = slots[bi]; slots[bi] = t; }
40	  g.player.inv.selected = 0;
41	  g.player.useTime = 0;
42	
43	  const res = {};
44	  // ---- 链条贴图加载 ----
45	  res.chain6 = await new Promise((resolve) => {
46	    const im = new Image();
47	    im.onload = () => resolve(im.naturalWidth > 0);
48	    im.onerror = () => resolve(false);
49	    im.src = 'sprites/vanilla/Chain6.png';
50	  });
51	
52	  // ---- 1) 按住:旋转态 ----
53	  g.input.mouseX = 640 + 300; g.input.mouseY = 400; // 光标在右侧
54	  g.input.mouseDown = true; g.input.mouseHeld = true;
55	  let flail = null;
56	  for (let i = 0; i < 40 && !flail; i++) {
57	    g.fixedUpdate(1 / 60);
58	    flail = g.entities.projectiles.find((p) => p.projId === 35 && !p.dead);
59	  }
60	  res.spawned = !!flail;
61	  if (flail) {
62	    res.spawnFrame = 'ok';
63	    // 旋转 60+ tick,采样球心距
64	    const dists = [];
65	    for (let i = 0; i < 70; i++) {
66	      g.fixedUpdate(1 / 60);
67	      const d = Math.hypot(flail.x + flail.w / 2 - g.player.cx, flail.y + flail.h / 2 - g.player.cy);
68	      dists.push(+d.toFixed(1));
69	    }
70	    res.orbitDists = { min: Math.min(...dists), max: Math.max(...dists), samples: dists.slice(0, 8) };
71	    res.aliveAfterSpin = !flail.dead;
72	    // 2) 静态图标:使用期间不应有 swing 持物动画
73	    res.swingNull = !g.swing || g.swing.item !== sunfuryId;
74	    // 2b) GetWeaponCrit:4 基础 + 武器 crit 7(Item.cs case 220) = 11%
75	    res.critChance = flail.critChance;
76	  }
77	
78	  // ---- 4) 灼烧:摆敌怪在旋转路径上,固定 Math.random=0.1(50% 掷骰必过) ----
79	  if (flail) {
80	    const en = g.spawnEnemy('v_6', g.player.cx + 30, g.player.cy - 20); // 僵尸(原版 6)
81	    const realRandom = Math.random;
82	    Math.random = () => 0.1;
83	    en.onFireT = 0;
84	    let fired = false;
85	    for (let i = 0; i < 240; i++) {
86	      g.fixedUpdate(1 / 60);
87	      if ((en.onFireT ?? 0) > 0) { fired = true; break; }
88	    }
89	    Math.random = realRandom;
90	    res.burnApplied = fired;
91	    res.enemyOnFire = +(en.onFireT ?? 0).toFixed(2);
92	    en.dead = true;
93	  }
94	
95	  // ---- 3) 松手:掷出(初速 17 type 35) ----
96	  if (flail && !flail.dead) {
97	    const x0 = flail.x, y0 = flail.y;
98	    g.input.mouseHeld = false;
99	    for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) g.fixedUpdate(1 / 60);
100	    res.throwV = { vx: +flail.vx.toFixed(1), vy: +flail.vy.toFixed(1), x: +flail.x.toFixed(0) };
101	    res.thrownRight = flail.vx > 14;
102	    // 再走 20 tick:掷出态直线
103	    for (let i = 0; i < 20; i++) g.fixedUpdate(1 / 60);
104	    res.aliveAfterThrow = !flail.dead;
105	  }
106	  return res;
107	});

120	check('命中施加 OnFire(StatusNPC)', out.burnApplied === true, `onFireT=${out.enemyOnFire}s`);
121	check('松手朝鼠标掷出(初速 17)', out.thrownRight === true, JSON.stringify(out.throwV));

1524	export class FlaironSpike extends Entity {
1525	  w = 14; h = 14;
1526	  damage: number;
1527	  knockback: number;
1528	  projId = 405;
1529	  critChance = 0.04;
1530	  meleeProj = true;
1531	  private ai0: number;   // -10 起步(1058 传参语义)
1532	  private ai1: number;   // scale
1533	  private alpha = 255;
1534	  private bounces = 0;
1535	  private hitCd = new HitCooldown();
1536	  life = 120;
1537	  dead = false;
1538	
1539	  constructor(x: number, y: number, vx: number, vy: number, damage: number, knockback: number) {
1540	    super();
1541	    this.x = x; this.y = y;
1542	    this.vx = vx; this.vy = vy;
1543	    this.damage = damage;
1544	    this.knockback = knockback;
1545	    this.ai0 = -10;
1546	    this.ai1 = (80 + Math.random() * 41) / 100; // Next(80,121)/100
1547	  }
1548	
1549	  draw(r: Renderer, cam: Camera): void {
1550	    // α 原版语义(Main.cs:28965 color *= (255-alpha)/255,越大越透明):SetDefaults 255
1551	    // (:4590)出生隐形,漂入段每 tick -25 至 50(:29225-29229)→ 淡入。
1552	    // 此前直接 alpha/255 当不透明度 = 方向写反(出生全显、越漂越透明)。
1553	    // scale=ai1(:29237)是贴图缩放非命中盒——此前改 w 会让绘制中心随缩放偏移
1554	    drawProj(r, cam, this, this.projId,
1555	      Math.atan2(this.vy, this.vx) + Math.PI / 2, '#FFB060',
1556	      Math.max(0, (255 - this.alpha) / 255), Math.round(14 * this.ai1));
1557	  }
1558	
1559	  fixedUpdate(_dt: number, game: GameHooks): void {
1560	    if (--this.life <= 0) { this.dead = true; return; }
1561	    this.hitCd.tick();
1562	    const cx0 = this.x + this.w / 2, cy0 = this.y + this.h / 2;
1563	    if (this.ai0 === 0) {
1564	      // 首寻:650px 内最近敌(距离+CanHit)
1565	      let best = 650, bx = 0, by = 0, found = false;
1566	      for (const ent of game.enemies()) {
1567	        const e = ent as { x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number; dead: boolean };
1568	        if (e.dead) continue;
1569	        if (!canHit(game.world.store, this.x, this.y, this.w, this.h, e.x, e.y, e.w, e.h)) continue;
1570	        const d = Math.hypot(e.x + e.w / 2 - cx0, e.y + e.h / 2 - cy0);
1571	        if (d < best) { best = d; bx = e.x + e.w / 2; by = e.y + e.h / 2; found = true; }
1572	      }
1573	      this.ai0 = found ? 1 : -15; // repo 无 NPC 索引,命中即锚定首敌(ai0=1)
1574	      if (found) {
1575	        const num605 = (35 + Math.random() * 40) / 30; // Next(35,75)/30
1576	        const jx = bx - cx0 + (Math.random() * 200 - 100);
1577	        const jy = by - cy0 + (Math.random() * 200 - 100);
1578	        const jd = Math.hypot(jx, jy) || 1;
1579	        this.vx = (this.vx * 20 + (jx / jd) * num605) / 21;
1580	        this.vy = (this.vy * 20 + (jy / jd) * num605) / 21;
1581	      }
